## Document 003: 1102亞泥 第一金

### Page 1

羅子評 
(886-2)2563-1666 
jason.luo@firstsec.com.tw

亞泥(1102) 
區間操作, TP 37 (潛在報酬 10.61%)  
區間操作 
Price(08/17)：33.45 
Target Price：37

亞泥 26Q2 單季營收達 178.31 億元 ，QoQ 20.24%，YoY -4.47%。
雖受中國煤炭成本攀升及報價低迷影響，拖累合併毛利率降至
14.03%；然受惠遠東新與裕民等業外轉投資挹注，單季EPS 仍逆
勢跳增至 1.57 元。展望後市，考量中國水泥市場呈 L 型築底且台
灣靜待房市鬆綁，本業延續承壓基調。研究部預估2026 全年 EPS
為 3.27 元；若以過往 65%配息率推估，2027 年有望配發 2.13 元
現金股利，配息後每股淨值約為 51.02 元。 
評價方面， 考量整體水泥產業仍處於下行階段 ， 且本業營收與獲利
持續承壓，給予過往區間中緣偏下之 0.7 倍 PBR 評價，目標價設
定為 37 元。惟業外獲利強勁有效彌補本業缺口，並提供配息下檔
保護；鑑於公司具備長期殖利率保護優勢，投資建議維持區間操
作，建議可逢低布局。  
⚫ 近期投資評等 ⚫ 公司基本資料 
亞洲水泥隸屬於遠東集團 ， 為台灣與中國具指標性之建材與多
角化控股公司。集團主要營運版圖涵蓋水泥、非水泥及策略轉
投資三大板塊。以近期營收結構分析，核心水泥本業合計貢獻
總營收將近七成，其中台灣市場約占近四成，中國市場約占三
成；非水泥事業則以電力領域為營運第二隻腳，穩定貢獻約兩
成營收，其餘則為不鏽鋼及其他業務。此外，亞泥亦透過權益
法戰略投資遠東新、裕民及山水水泥等關聯企業，建構出具防
禦性且多角化的穩健獲利基礎。

⚫ 中國水泥事業低迷，惟業外貢獻支撐 26Q2 EPS 至 1.57 元 
26Q2 工作天數增加，台灣水泥出貨季增；但中國市場報價偏
低且需求年減，使集團單季營收達 178.31 億元， QoQ 
20.24%，YoY -4.47%。獲利方面，因中國煤炭成本攀升與終
端報價低迷，中國事業噸毛利由 25Q2 的 40 元人民幣降至約
15 元 ， 並轉為虧損 ， 拖累合併毛利率降至14.03%， 年減4.04
個百分點，季增 1.02 個百分點，營業利益 17.11 億元，QoQ 
44.52%，YoY -34.09%。惟業外受惠遠東新與裕民轉投資支
撐，彌補本業缺口，其中權益法投資認列13.95 億元，金融資
產評價利益 26.58 億元。單季稅後獲利跳增至 52.27 億元，
QoQ 176.98%，YoY 78.40%，EPS 達 1.57 元。

⚫ 公司基本資料

第一金證券投資顧問股份有限公司(First Capital Management Inc.) 台北市長安東路一段 35 號 3F   TEL(02) 2563 -1666 
營業執照字號:114 金管投顧新字第 013 號 
2 
 ⚫ 本業展望保守，惟業外貢獻支撐配息下檔保護 
展望後市，隨中國房市未見起色，台灣建築市場仍以存量消耗
為主，亞泥下半年整體營運將延續本業築底、轉投資彌補整體
獲利之基調。預期公司全年 EPS 為 3.27 元，按照過往 65%
配息率推估，預估 2027 可配發 2.13 元現金股利。

水泥：中國 L 型築底，台灣靜待房市鬆綁 
中國市場受房地產需求量下行與市場報價維持低位，需求呈L
型復甦。亞泥中國採取保市佔策略，預估全年出貨量約下滑
2%， 幅度低於中國平均約10%衰退 。 展望下半年 ， 預期26Q3
報價持平，但受煤炭成本維持高位盤整，預期26Q3 噸毛利恐
略低於 26Q2，全年噸毛利預估落於 15 元人民幣附近。台灣
市場上半年受房市信用管制及土方新制干擾，需求量下滑約
15%。然台灣水泥報價維持平穩，且預拌混凝土事業受惠於科
技廠辦需求支撐，毛利率表現相對穩健。下半年隨政策底部逐
漸浮現與剛性買盤進場，後續將持續觀察開工與施工進度。

資料來源:亞洲水泥 26Q2 法說、第一金投顧匯整

非水泥事業：嘉惠具基本盤，不鏽鋼維持損平 
嘉惠電力下半年營運趨勢預期與 26Q2 相當 ， 主要受國際天然
氣價格走揚使成本承壓，預期營利呈小幅年減。但受惠與台電
的保證發電量合約機制，嘉惠仍具獲利下檔支撐，後續成長空
間端視氣價與台電調度、增購需求而定。遠龍不鏽鋼事業則因
終端市況偏弱，加上海外原料採購及庫存調節具波動性，預期
下半年獲利將維持於損益兩平線波動。 
業外貢獻支撐，預估 2026 全年 EPS 為 3.27 元 
綜上所述，儘管短期兩岸水泥本業面臨逆風，但業外貢獻仍對
合併獲利提供支撐，研究部預估 2026 亞泥全年營收 673.20
億，YoY -5.23%；毛利率 13.67%，年減 2.16 個百分點；
營業利益 61.14 億，YoY -27.27%； 稅後獲利 111.42 億，YoY 
11.09%；EPS 為 3.27 元；配息後每股淨值約為 51.02 元。

雖受中國煤炭成本攀升及報價低迷影響，拖累合併毛利率降至
14.03%；然受惠遠東新與裕民等業外轉投資強勢挹注，單季 EPS
仍逆勢跳增至 1.57 元 。 展望後市 ， 考量中國水泥市場呈L 型築底且
台灣靜待房市鬆綁 ， 本業延續承壓基調 。 研究部預估2026 全年 EPS
為 3.27 元；若以過往 65%配息率推估，2027 年有望配發 2.13 元
現金股利，配息後每股淨值約為 51.02 元。
評價方面，考量整體水泥產業仍處於下行階段，且本業營收與獲利
持續承壓 ， 給予過往區間中緣偏下之0.7 倍 PBR 評價 ， 目標價設定
為 37 元。惟業外獲利強勁有效彌補本業缺口，並提供配息下檔保

護；鑑於公司具備長期殖利率保護優勢，投資建議維持區間操作，
建議可逢低布局。

## Document 014: 1513中興電 國票

Source PDF: all report pdf/1513中興電 國票.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-08-18

### Page 1

請參閱末頁之聲明

2026.08.18 
吳昆潮 C00269@ibfs.com.tw 
1513.TT 中興電 
 財報優於預期，在手訂單充裕

[財務資訊更新]   
   公司簡介 
中興電是台灣電機設備製造商，為台灣電力基礎建設的重要供應商，重電產品以直接
銷售方式售予台電公司、公共設施事業及各大工廠，台電是單一最大客戶。  
投資評等/目標價 重點摘要 
買進 198 
【 前 次 投 資 建 議 】買進，195 
【大盤指數/股價】45857 / 171

➢ 2Q26 EPS 2.56 元，獲利優於預期 
中興電 2Q26 營收 75.33 億元，QoQ+16.06%，YoY+6.03%，稅後淨
利 12.68 億元，QoQ+35.52%，YoY+34.65%，EPS 2.56 元，優於投研
部預期，主要受惠台電強韌電網計畫、3-1 期離岸風電及半導體擴廠，
產品組合優於預期 ， 毛利率回升至28.44%， 優於投研部預期的26.15%。 
➢ 上修資本支出以因應產能需求 
中興電因應目前產能滿載 、 且部分長單能見度直達2031 年的暢旺需求 ，
2026 年的資本支出上修至 15 至 20 億元 ， 除了用來鞏固GIS 升級測試 、
生質能設備代理與 BOT 案等綠能本業外，並投入 AI 系統與設備建置，
中興電預計透過導入「自動化焊接」與「AI 報價系統」雙管齊下，精進
製造與商務效率，目標在 2026 年將整體產能拉升 15%到 20%，全力消
化手頭訂單。 
➢ 截至 3 月底在手訂單達 431 億元，營運穩健 
中興電在氣體絕緣開關具絕對的領 先優勢，在台電與非台電體系市佔率
達 80%及 65%。隨著全球能源轉型與 AI 算力崛起，中興電全面受惠於
「台電強韌電網計畫」 、離岸風電與半導體海內外擴廠；同時，國際大廠
積極建置 AI 與 IDC 資料中心，更引爆龐大的重電設備剛性需求，中興
電憑藉壓倒性的技術實力與市場份額，將全面受惠海內外建設紅利，持
續驅動 GIS 業績穩定成長，其中 GIS 已取得國內晶圓半導體大廠認證，
預計 4Q26 開始出貨。截至 2026 年 3 月底，中興電在重電事業新接訂
單金額 63 億元，在手訂單累計 431 億元，而台電強韌電網計畫及半導
體擴廠專案的延續性佳，未來中興電接單及營運動能穩健。 
➢ 預估 2027 年 EPS 10.21 元，維持買進評等，目標價 198 元 
投研部預估中興電 2026 年營收 292.29 億元，YoY+6.49%，預估稅後
淨利 46.07 億元，YoY+15.52%，稅後 EPS 9.32 元；預估 2027 年營收
317.90 億元，YoY+8.76%，稅後淨利 50.45 億元，YoY+9.50%，EPS 
10.21 元。國內台電穩定釋單，訂單能見度佳，且 GIS 設備正式打入國
內半導體大廠供應鏈 ， 有利評價拉升 ， 目前PE 16.7X， 位於PE 15X~25X
區間下緣，維持買進評等，目標價 198 元(2027F PER 19X)。

1513 中興電 加權指數

### Page 2

- 2 -

個股報告

公司簡介 
中興電是台灣電機設備製造商，為台灣電力基礎建設的重要供應商，重電產品以直接
銷售方式售予台電公司、公共設施事業及各大工廠，台電是單一最大客戶，佔中興電
營收比重約 50%，其中 345kV 氣體絕緣開關設備(GIS)，是國內唯一獲得台電認證的
本土廠商，而69kV 以上高壓開關在國內的市佔率達 70％，變電所的統包工程市佔率
37%。中興電主要以國內市場為主，內銷佔營收比重約 91%。 
圖 1：中興電營收比重

資料來源：中興電，國票投顧整理 
2Q26 EPS 2.56 元，獲利優於預期  
中興電 2Q26 營收 75.33 億元 ，QoQ+16.06%，YoY+6.03%，稅後淨利 12.68 億元，
QoQ+35.52%，YoY+34.65%，EPS 2.56 元，優於投研部預期，主要受惠台電強韌電
網計畫、3-1 期離岸風電及半導體擴廠，產品組合優於預期，毛利率回升至 28.44%，
優於投研部預期的 26.15%。 
資料來源：國票投顧整理

### Page 3

- 3 -

個股報告 
截至 3 月底在手訂單達 431 億元，合約負債金額維持高檔 
中興電在氣體絕緣開關(GIS)具有絕對的領先優勢， 在台電與非台電體系市佔率達80%
及 65%。隨著全球能源轉型與 AI 算力崛起， 中興電全面受惠於 「台電強韌電網計畫」 、
離岸風電與半導體海內外擴廠；同時，國際大廠積極建置 AI 與 IDC 資料中心，更引
爆龐大的重電設備剛性需求，中興電憑藉壓倒性的技術實力與市場份額，將全面受惠
海內外建設紅利，持續驅動 GIS 業績穩定成長，其中 GIS 已取得國內晶圓半導體大廠
認證，預計 4Q26 開始出貨。截至 2026 年 3 月底，中興電在重電事業新接訂單金額
63 億元，在手訂單累計 431 億元；至 6 月底，公司合約負債金額 89 億元，維持高
檔，台電強韌電網計畫及半導體擴廠專案的延續性佳，未來中興電接單及營運動能穩
健。 
圖 2：中興電合約負債金額

資料來源：CMoney，國票投顧整理 
上修資本支出以因應產能需求 
中興電因應目前產能滿載 、 且部分長單能見度直達2031 年的暢旺需求 ，2026 年的資
本支出上修至 15 至 20 億元，除了用來鞏固 GIS 升級測試、生質能設備代理與 BOT
案等綠能本業外，並投入 AI 系統與設備建置，中興電預計透過導入 「自動化焊接」 與
「AI 報價系統」 雙管齊下 ， 精進製造與商務效率 ， 目標在2026 年將整體產能拉升 15%
到 20%，全力消化手頭訂單。 
預估 2027 年 EPS 10.21 元，維持買進評等，目標價 198 元 
投研部預估中興電 2026 年營收 292.29 億元，YoY+6.49%，預估稅後淨利 46.07 億
元，YoY+15.52%， 稅後EPS 9.32 元 ；預估2027 年營收 317.90 億元，YoY+8.76%，
稅後淨利 50.45 億元，YoY+9.50%，EPS 10.21 元。國內台電穩定釋單，訂單能見度
佳，且 GIS 設備正式打入國內半導體大廠供應鏈，有利評價拉升，目前 PE 16.7X，位
於 PE 15X~25X 區間下緣，維持中興電買進評等，目標價 198 元(2027F PER 19X)。

股價相對大盤走勢

和成 (1810 TT)  Buy

無人機載具出貨台系廠商。  
投資建議 ：軍工國防產品放量出貨，加上本業衛浴系統穩定成
長，預估 2026 年軍工營收比重約 10% 、 2027 年有望提升至逾
15% 。 08/14/2026 立法院正式三讀通過 6 年合計約台幣 442 億
元之無人載具產業發展統籌型計畫預算案，以及行政院各部會
編列之 192 億元採購預算，全力支持台灣國防安全事業。目前
股價處於評價面 PBR 歷史區間 0.4X~1.3X 中間值。給予投資建
議 Buy ，目標價 34 元 (2026 年 PBR 1.18X) 。  
精密陶瓷事業，佈局國防領域，推出 RF3 等級抗彈板 ：積極佈
局國防領域。現階段 RF2 等級抗彈板持續出貨中，通路經銷商
等期望公司加速最高等級 RF3 抗彈板研發並供貨。因供給相對
有限，需求提升，預估該產品毛利表現佳。  
強化碳纖維複合材料，運用於無人機載具： 開發碳纖維複合材
料，主要運用於無人機之機殼與輪圈，且 2025 年已開始出貨。
無人機載具產品，主要出貨給漢翔、雷虎等台系廠商，配合開
發無人機、無人艇外殼材料。和成與雷虎共同開發小型、中型
無人機機身之碳纖維複合材料，正放量出貨中。  
立法院正式通過無人載具 634 億元預算案： 08/14/2026 立法院
正式三讀通過 6 年合計約台幣 442 億元之無人載具產業發展統
籌型計畫預算案，以及行政院各部會編列之 192 億元採購預算，
總金額約 634 億元，展現強化台灣國防安全企圖心。  
2026/2027 年軍工產品挹注，碳纖複合材料持續出貨： 2026 年
預估本業衛浴設備持平 2025 年，增加無人機載具貢獻，預估軍
工營收比重約 10%，2027 年預期本業有望較 2026 年微幅成長，
且 2027 年軍工營收比重有望提升至逾 15% 。研究部預估 2026

2 群益投顧 
  和成 ESG ：  
和成 HCG 集團於 08/12/2025 上傳申報並公告 2024 年度 ESG 永續報告書。
永續經營方面 ， 針對溫室氣體報告以 2024 年度為基準年自主揭露營運概況 ，
以降低溫室氣體排放為目標，汰換柴 油堆高機為電動堆高機，預計 2027 年
底前更新柴油設備 4 台為電動堆高機，減少碳排放量約 18 公噸 /年。以善盡
企業社會責任為己任，榮獲第 8 屆台灣陶藝金陶獎，並舉辦后里森林復育計
畫。節水衛浴系統方面，取得 2025 年金點新秀設計獎，並馳援花蓮馬太鞍
溪堰塞湖災害援助，提供馬桶、臉盆及水龍頭等衛浴設備， 2.5~6 折優惠；
品牌永續方面，旗下包含和成三廠，以及台北、台中、台南、高雄等 4 大展
示中心，並於馬尼拉設立旗艦展示中心。  
公司簡介：  
和成成立於 12/09/1961 ，並於 10/14/1991 掛牌上市。主要產品包括各種衛浴
設備及周邊產品生產銷售，並積極佈局精密陶瓷事業、強化碳纖複合材料等
新材料事業。生產基地有桃園、新北、中國江蘇及菲律賓馬尼拉。  
2026 年超配現金股利：  
經董事會通過，和成 2026 年配發 2025 年度每股現金股利 0.2 元，現金配發
率達 400% 。

強化碳纖維複合材料，運用於無人機載具：  
和成除持續深耕本業衛浴系統外，為強化轉型動能，積極開發碳纖維複合材
料，主要運用於無人機之機殼與輪圈， 2023 年陸續生產，且 2025 年已開始
出貨。無人機載具產品，主要出貨給漢翔、雷虎等台系廠商，配合開發無人
機、無人艇外殼材料。和成與雷虎共同開發小型、中型無人機機身之碳纖維
複合材料，正放量出貨中，且和成亦提供高純度氧化鋁、碳化矽等陶瓷產品，
配合雷虎生產無人艇引擎之遮蔽熱源及抗雷達偵測設備，且處於測試過程。
為打入半導體擴建廠房商機，亦提供無塵室格子樑及鋪設於店面之導管，以
SMC/ 熱壓成型製程製造。受惠日本大廠 TOTO 打入半導體供應鏈，可望為
具備精密陶瓷及高溫燒結技術，身兼半導體級製程關鍵材料技術之和成營
運添磚加瓦，可運用於半導體產業與國防裝備上，增添打入半導體設備關鍵
零組件供應鏈想像。

4 群益投顧 
立法院正式通過無人載具 634 億元預算案：  
08/14/2026 立法院正式三讀通過 6 年合計約台幣 442 億元之無人載具產業
發展統籌型計畫預算案，以及行政院各部會編列之 192 億元採購預算，總金
額約 634 億元，展現強化台灣國防安全企圖心。外貿協會董事長更表示無人
機產業馬上要起飛，且將持續協助相關業者開拓海外營運版圖。無人機事業
相關供應商，包括雷虎、漢翔、中光電、長榮航太等，而和成主要提供上述
廠商無人機、無人艇之碳纖維複合材料。  
1Q26 認列搬遷補償利益 14.33 億元，單季獲利超越 2025 年：  
和成 1Q26 營收 10.35 億元， QoQ-7.74% 、YoY-8.14% ；營費毛利 1.80 億元，
毛利率約 17.36% ；業外收支 14.13 億元，主因認列蘇州潤淞開發建設之搬
遷補償利益 14.33 億元、租金股利及權利金等收入 1,850 萬元，以及其他利
益與損失 4,198.7 萬元；稅後純益 10.98 億元， QoQ+31,877.38% 、 YoY 虧轉
盈，稅後 EPS 3.63 元。  
2Q26 受惠軍工產品出貨增加，毛利率大幅提升至 25.40% ：  
和成 2Q26 營收 11.47 億元， QoQ+10.81% 、 YoY-8.28% ；營費毛利 2.91 億
元，毛利率約 25.40% ，主因軍工產品出貨量增加，營收比重同步提升；惟
業外收支因 1Q26 基期較高，大幅下滑，使得稅後虧損 6 百萬元， QoQ 盈轉
虧、 YoY 盈轉虧，稅後 EPS -0.02 元。  
2026/2027 年軍工產品挹注，碳纖複合材料持續出貨：  
2026 年主要營運動能，包括衛浴本業、無人機載具及半導體晶圓載具、軌
道工程產品開發等。預估本業衛浴設備持平 2025 年，將持續強化整體衛浴
UB 產品，增加無人機載具貢獻，預估軍工營收比重約 10%。2027 年受惠營
建產業可望落底逐步回升，預期本業有望較 2026 年微幅成長，且 2027 年軍
工產品營收比重有望提升至逾 15% 。研究部預估 2026 年營收 46.83 億元，
YoY+2.76% ，稅後純益 12.44 億元， YoY+8,597.62% ，稅後 EPS 4.12 元；
2027 年營收 52.36 億元， YoY+11.82% ，稅後純益 2.74 億元， YoY-77.98% ，
稅後 EPS 0.91 元。  
投資建議：  
軍工國防產品放量出貨，加上本業衛 浴系統穩定成長，預估 2026 年軍工營
收比重約 10% 、 2027 年有望提升至逾 15% 。 08/14/2026 立法院正式三讀通
過 6 年合計約台幣 442 億元之無人載具產業發展統籌型計畫預算案，以及
行政院各部會編列之 192 億元採購預算，全力支持台灣國防安全事業。目前
股價處於評價面 PBR 歷史區間 0.4X~1.3X 中間值。給予投資建議 Buy ，目
標價 34 元 (2026 年 PBR1.18X) 。

8 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌，亦將出具降評報告。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

 評論及分析 (Overall Summary) 
Applied Materials, Inc. (AMAT) 2026 財年第三季 (Q3 FY2026) 財報展現公司
受惠於全球 AI 基礎設施的快速建設與對先進晶片需求激增的強勁動能。憑藉
在 AI 運算相關高效能與高價值技術領域的領導地位，公司實現創紀錄的單季
營收與獲利，並創下歷史最高的季增長率。在半導體系統與全球服務業務  
(AGS) 雙引擎發力推動下，單季營收達到 91 億美元。儘管面臨建廠無塵室空
間受限與短期擴產的人力成本挑戰，但客戶為確保供應鏈穩定，積極提供長
達八個季度的滾動預測，針對技術藍圖的能見度甚至延伸至 2030 年。隨著業
界迅速朝向先進邏輯製程、 DRAM 及先進封裝  (Advanced Packaging) 發
展，Applied Materials 透過其深厚的材料工程技術與 EPIC 創新平台，已進一
步鞏固其市場領導地位。展望 2026 財年第四季，在 DRAM 強勁復甦與領先
晶圓代工邏輯製程推動下，公司預期營收將再次大幅跳升，持續作為 AI 發展
趨勢下的主要受惠者。 
公司財報重點數據： 
Applied Materials 在 2026 財年第三季展現強勁的財務表現，單季總營收達 
91 億美元，不僅季增 15%，年增更達 25%，創下歷史新高。受惠於價值定價 
(Value-based pric ing) 策略及高利潤產品組合， Non-GAAP 毛利率微升至  
50.4%，較上季成長  40 個基點，較去年同期增加  150 個基點；同時  Non-
GAAP 營業利益率也擴張至 34% 的歷史新高，年增 330 個基點。亮眼的營運
表現帶動第三季 Non-GAAP 每股盈餘 (EPS) 達 3.50 美元（+41%YoY）。 
公司業績表現： 
⚫ 半導體系統 (Semiconductor Systems) 業務：營收達創紀錄的 70 億美元 
(+27%YoY)。其中，DRAM 營收 (包含 HBM 封裝) 較去年同期增長 52% 
創下新高。在沉積 (包含 PVD、CVD 及磊晶)、材料改質、材料移除 (蝕刻
與 CMP) 以及製程診斷與控制 (PDC) 等多個領域皆創下營收紀錄。該部門 
Non-GAAP 毛利率升至 55.4%，Non-GAAP 營業利益達 27 億美元。 
⚫ 全球服務 (Applied Global Services, AGS) 業務：營收達 18 億美元 (年增 
22%)。受惠於訂閱服務成長與高交易性零件需求，並利用  AI 與倉儲自動
化提升營運效率，部門毛利率達 35.6%，營業利益率達 30.1%。目前全球
已有超過 37,000 個機台連接至其專屬的 AIx 軟體平台。 
⚫ 其他業務 (Other)：營收約 2.94 億美元，主要為顯示器 (Display) 業務。

複委託報告 August 14, 2026

### Page 2

2
公司展望與產業前景：
⚫ 2026 財政年度第四季指引 ：預估營收將落在  102.5 億美元區間  (+
51%YoY)。半導體系統營收預計約 79 億美元 (+62%YoY)，AGS 營收約
18.4 億美元  (年增  22%)。Non-GAAP 毛利率預估約  50.4%，Non-
GAAP EPS 預計為 4.02 美元 (+85%YoY)。
⚫ 先進封裝與 PDC 前景：預期 2026 日曆年的整體先進封裝營收將成長超
過 70%，而製程診斷與控制 (PDC) 業務在今年也將成長超過 50%。
⚫ 產能擴張與中國市場 ：為了應對強勁的市場需求，公司本季新增超過
1,500 名製造與客服人員，並計畫在 2028 年前將系統產出能力翻倍。中
國市場本季佔半導體系統與 AGS 總營收的 26%，在 28 奈米代工邏輯投
資推動下，預期今年將持續成長。
電話會議 Q&A 重點摘要：
⚫ 訂單能見度與長期合約 ：管理層指出，針對先進晶片供不應求的情況，
大型客戶提供了長達八個季度的詳細滾動式預測，部分關鍵客戶針對技

術藍圖的能見度更延伸至  2030 年。這種高能見度顯著改善了公司與供
應鏈的產能規劃。
⚫ 毛利率與定價策略動態 ：分析師關注毛利率在營收大幅增長下於下一季
度的指引為何持平。管理層解釋，短期內因大量招募工程師與服務人員
產生了「爬坡成本」(Ramp costs) 抵銷了部分規模經濟優勢。然而，透
過「價值定價」(Value-based pricing) 策略，半導體系統毛利率已穩居
55% 以上，長期來看整體毛利率仍將保持增長趨勢。
⚫ ICAPS 與各終端市場復甦：針對 ICAPS ( 物聯網、通訊、汽車、電源與
感測器) 市場，公司預期該領域的設備消化期已接近尾聲，隨著客戶端
產能利用率上升以及電源、光子晶片的亮點需求，預期 ICAPS 整體在今
年及明年皆將重返成長軌道。
公司簡介：
Applied Materials, Inc. 是全球領先的半導體與顯示器設備及材料工程解決方
案供應商。該公司致力於推動資料中心、人工智慧  (AI) 運算基礎架構與先進
應用的底層晶片製造技術。其廣泛的產品線涵蓋沉積、材料移除、先進封裝

至製程診斷控制系統 (PDC)，並透過 Applied Global Services (AGS) 提供高
度依賴  AI 預 測 分 析 的 機 台 最 佳 化 解 決 方 案 。 近 期 更 透 過 設 立 EPIC
(Equipment and Process Innovation and Commercialization) 中心，進一
步深化與頂尖晶片製造商及研究機構的早期共同創新 (Co-innovation)。
競爭對手與公司自身價值：
Applied Materials 在全球半導體製造設備市場中面臨眾多同業的競爭。相較
於競爭對手， AMAT 的核心價值與護城河在於其最廣泛且高度互補的技術組
合 (特別在先進邏輯製程、DRAM 及先進封裝)。該公司透過全新的 EPIC 平台
讓客戶在架構發展的極早期階段便能接觸最新設備創新，從而加速新技術導
入並提升晶圓廠的生產良率與單位面積產出。這種深度的技術整合與協助客
戶提升廠區投資報酬率的能力，使其在 AI 軍備競賽中擁有無可取代的產業地

位。

### Page 6

Lotes  (3533.TW)
14 August 2026 Citi Research
6
Lotes
Company description
Established in 1986 and listed in 2007, Lotes is a leading solution provider of
connectors and cable assembly services based in Keelung, Taiwan. The
company runs a fully integrated business model, with product design, tooling,
molding, stamping, electro-plating and automation process all under the
same roof. Majority of Lotes’ manufacturing capacity is located in China,
Guangzhou, Huanan, and Suzhou, with headquarters located in Keelung,

Taiwan. The main focus areas include CPU socket/ILM and connectors for
DDR, PCIe, SATA, Type-C, and etc. Specifically for CPU socket, Lotes is now
the No.2 supplier globally with 25%+ of global market share. Lotes also owns
3,200+ patents globally for product design and manufacturing process
protection, which has raised the bar for new competition. Lotes started the
business as a PC connector supplier but has shifted its focus to server and
other non-PC business in recent years. Management targets to increase its

non-PC related exposure 70% to total sales in next 5 years, vs 56% in 2018.
Investment strategy
We rate Lotes as Buy. Lotes has c25%+ global market share in CPU sockets
and is seeing rising exposure to servers. We expect Lotes to grow its sales and
earnings at 16% and 27% CAGR in 2023-25E, backed by server sales growth,
CPU platform migration and spec migration in sockets. We forecast server-
related sales to account for 30-35% of total sales in 2024-25E, vs. 28% in

2023. As server products generate better margins than PCs, we expect
margins to improve based on product mix changes and better manufacturing
yield as well as cost efficiency. We also expect CPU platform transition and
spec migration of connectors to lead to ASP increases amid limited
competition.
Valuation
Our target price for Lotes is NT$2,500. We value Lotes based on PE as we
believe the company’s earnings performance and industry dynamics would be

the key focus of the market. Our target price is based on 20x 2027E EPS,
which is at the higher-end of the historical trading range since 2015 and is
justified by the company’s successful transformation from a PC-centric
component maker to a company with increasing server exposure (posing
higher entry barriers), robust earnings growth and margin expansion, as well
as rising ROE.
Risks
Key downside risks that could cause Lotes shares to trade below our target

price include: 1) Potential delays in CPU launch schedule; 2) Peer competition;
3) Raw material price hike; 4) Patent litigation; and 5) FX volatility.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk

rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment
rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered

stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating
and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also

suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price
chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in

exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the
expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility

or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:
Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside

call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is
moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day

period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be
different from and does not affect a stock’s fundamental equity rating, which reflects a longer-term total absolute return

2Q26 GM beat: AVC’s 2Q26 revenue was up 66% YoY, largely in line with our 
estimate and Bloomberg consensus. GM delivered strong QoQ and YoY 
improvement, reaching 32.6%, which we attribute to a rising contribution from AI 
server liquid cooling components, which carry a higher GM than other product lines.

### Page 2

The 2Q26 opex ratio improved to 5.1%, better than our estimate and Bloomberg 
consensus; along with the higher-than-expected non-op income, this led 2Q26 net 
income to come in 23% / 11% higher than our estimate / Bloomberg consensus.

Earnings revisions: We factor in AVC’s Jul revenue and 2Q26 results and raise our 
2026-28E net incomes by 11% / 7% / 8%, mainly on higher revenues and GMs. Our 
2026-28E revenues are revised up by 1% / 2% / 2%, mainly on a higher contribution 
from AI server liquid cooling components. 2026-28E GMs are revised up to reflect the 
2Q26 GM beat, the rising complexity of liquid cooling components driving profitability, 
and AVC’s rising adoption of automation equipment. 2026-28E opex ratios are largely 
unchanged.

Valuation: We continue to use a near-term P/E to derive our 12-month target price and 
update our target P/E multiple to 28.2x 2027E EPS (vs. 26.7x previously), which is still 
derived from PC/server peers’ EPS growth vs. P/E multiple correlation. Our target P/E 
multiple is above AVC’s historical average +1stv. forward trading P/E, reflecting our 
positive view on AVC riding on the rising penetration rate of liquid-cooled AI servers. 
With our updated target P/E and earnings estimates, we raise our 12-month target price 
to NT$3,986. Maintain Buy.

### Page 3

Price Target Risks and Methodology - AVC 
We are Buy-rated on AVC. Our 12-month target price for AVC is based on a near-term 
P/E, consistent with our Taiwan Technology coverage. We base our target price of 
NT$3,986 on a target P/E multiple of 28.2x applied to our forward-year EPS (2027E). 
Our target P/E is derived from the correlation between P/E and EPS growth of its peers 
in technology hardware.  
Key downside risks: (1) slower-than-expected adoption of liquid cooling; (2) 
lower-than-expected demand for AI servers; (3) fiercer-than-expected competition; 
and (4) a slower-than-expected recovery in general servers.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

### Page 3

Investment Thesis and Price Target Risks and Methodology

Investment thesis - Hiwin Corp 
Hiwin is the second-largest global linear motion supplier with 18% market share in 2024. 
It continues to witness decelerating market share growth from 2020 when it started to 
pursue a deleveraging strategy.   
Although we expect Hiwin to continue to benefit from rising demand from the 
semiconductor/EV industry given the upgrading trend, we are Sell rated, given: (1) we 
expect a weak end-market demand recovery and high inventory levels for the near term, 
leading to high pricing pressure and a stagnant utilization rate; (2) continued 
overcapacity issues in mainland China, where the company has higher exposure (45% in 
2024) vs peers such as THK (24%), and we expect this would lead to further 
deterioration in Hiwin’s revenue/profitability outlook in the near term; and (3) the 
negative impact from its long-term deleveraging strategy. Hiwin continues to pay back 
long-term debt and expand capacity to better compete with peers, which we believe will 
further impact EPS growth and valuation. The stock is currently trading above its 
historical average valuation (33.0x). We expect its P/E multiple to derate once the 
market begins to adjust for a prolonged downcycle and expect downgrades to 
consensus EPS growth/ROE estimates, suggesting high downside risk to Hiwin’s 
valuation.  
Price target risks and methodology - Hiwin Corp 
Valuation methodology: Our 12m TP of NT$160 is based on a target P/E of 20x 
4Q26-3Q27E EPS (in-line with Hiwin’s average downcycle valuation, or 1std lower than 
the historical average P/E for the past 10 years).

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.
Coverage Universe: A list of all stocks in each coverage universe is available by primary analyst, stock and coverage universe at

https://www.gs.com/research/hedge.html.

SiPh capacity strong expansion through 2028E: Management targets capacity to 
grow at triple digit YoY growth in 2027/ 28E, riding on strong clients’ AI demand, 
rising SiPh penetration, also company’s capabilities to produce high-speed products. 
In mid June, Landmark announced the company has obtained equipment from 
Aixtron with total value at ~NT$837m, supporting its capacity expansion across InP 
Epi-Wafer and CW lasers. 
3-month preview: Landmark Jul revenues were up 177% YoY/ 19% MoM to 
NT$501m, or 14% higher than our previous estimates, reflecting strong demand on 
800G / 1.6T SiPh optical modules, better InP substrate supply, and Landmark’s 
capacity expansion. Looking ahead, we expect company’s Aug/ Sep revenues to 
grow at 173%/ 171% YoY to NT$506m/ NT$505m, supporting 3Q26E revenues to 
grow 174% YoY, or 24% QoQ to NT$1.5bn.

Earnings revision: We factor in Landmark 2Q26 result and revise up earnings by 4%/ 
1%/ 1%/ 1% in 2026-29E mainly on higher revenues of SiPh CW lasers, supported by 
strong client demand and capacity expansion. We revise up GM by 0.2ppts to 0.7ppts in 
2026-29E on improving product mix and larger scale.

Valuation: We employ a discounted P/E methodology (unchanged) and apply a 61.5x 
(vs. previously at 61.0x) target P/E multiple on our 2029E EPS, discounted back to 
2027E, at a COE of 10.5% (beta 1.7x, risk free rate 1.6%, and market risk premium at 
5.1% - unchanged). Our target P/E multiple of 61.5x (vs. previously at 61.0x) is derived 
from the PEG&M ratio of peers (unchanged) in the photonics supply chain, reflecting an 
industry re-rating of product mix upgrade and rising demand on optical solution. 
Consequently, our 12-month target price is now NT$4,401 (vs. previously at NT$4,307). 
The implied 2028E P/E at 76x is between company’s avg. P/E at 56x and avg. +1 P/E at 
82x. Maintain Buy.

### Page 4

Price Target Risks and Methodology - LandMark Optoelectronics Corp.  
Valuation: We employ a discounted P/E methodology, applying a 61.5x target P/E 
multiple on our 2029E EPS, then discount it back to 2027E at a COE of 10.5% (beta 1.7x, 
risk free rate 1.6% and market risk premium at 5.1%), to derive our 12-month target 
price of NT$4,401. Our target P/E multiple is based on the same PEG&M ratio as peers in 
the photonics supply chain. We are Buy-rated on LandMark. 
Key risk: Slower-than-expected SiPh penetration; stronger-than-expected competition; 
Integrated Device Manufacturers (IDMs) offering Epitaxial (EPI) wafers in-house.

Source: Company data, Goldman Sachs Research estimates, FactSet. Price as of 12 Aug 2026 close.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.
Coverage Universe: A list of all stocks in each coverage universe is available by primary analyst, stock and coverage universe at

https://www.gs.com/research/hedge.html.

GS Factor Profile  ______________________________

2Q26 beat on better opex control 
MPI reported its 2Q26 preliminary results (see Exhibit 1) on 12 Aug. 
2Q26 GM of 58.4% (59.4% 1Q26) came in slightly higher vs. GSe of 
57.9%, but inline with cons. of 58.5%. Meanwhile, 2Q26 OpM of 
34.4% exceeded both GSe/cons. of 32.1%/32.9%, driven by better 
cost control and stronger operating leverage. Overall, 2Q26 EPS of 
NT$15.55 came in above GSe and cons. by 7-10%. To note, the 
company will host an in-person analyst meeting on 14 Aug at 2pm 
Taiwan time. 
Set for GM improvement into 3Q26 and 4Q26 
Looking into 2H26, management now guides for 3Q26 revenue 
growth of 0-5% QoQ, followed by a 5-10% QoQ increase in 4Q26. 
We believe the sequential growth into 4Q26 should still be largely 
driven by the continued AI/HPC demand across both VPC and 
MEMS. To note, we expect MPI’s new US CPU customer could start 
to ramp in 4Q26. On the margin front, management expects GM to 
recover toward 1Q26 levels in 3Q26, driven by a more favorable 
product mix and price hikes (which happened in 2Q26, with positive 
effect contributing starting 3Q26), and 4Q26 GM is also now guided 
to be flat QoQ, but does not exclude the possibility for potential 
upside from new customer ramp and higher utilization rates. 
Meanwhile, management maintained its view that the 2026 opex 
ratio should remain within 25-27%, while expecting further 
operating leverage in 2027 despite continued investments in R&D 
and labor with capacity expansion. Overall, we now model 
3Q26E/4Q26E revenue growth of 10.7%/14.3% QoQ, with GM of 
59.8%/60.4%. 
Multiple growth drivers into 2027 
We expect MPI to deliver a strong revenue growth outlook with

continuous margin expansion into 2027E driven by 1) strong growth outlook from AI 
ASIC/networking customers with continuous TAM expansion, 2) significant market 
share gain within new US CPU customer, with the new customer potentially 
becoming the largest single customer in 2027, 3) the ramp of in-house PCB 
production in 3Q27, which could help lower its outsourcing ratio to 50% and help 
improve its profitability, 4) potential further price hike, and 5) emerging CPO 
opportunities. We now expect its revenue to grow by 105% YoY (vs. 61% YoY in 
2026E) with GM improving to 62.2% (vs. 59.6% in 2026E) in 2027E. 
CPO progressing well but qualification takes time 
In terms of the progress on CPO, MPI’s equipment for Insertion 3 are expected to begin 
engineering-sample shipments by around 10 units in 4Q26 to multiple customers 
spanning OSAT, foundry, and fabless clients. As for Insertion 2, MPI has completed 
on-site qualification and is now shipping to clients’ overseas production sites to do 
qualification. Overall, we remain optimistic that CPO will become the standard for 
next-generation AI networking, and we now model MPI’s CPO-related business to 
potentially contribute mid single digit % and high single digit % of its total revenue 
in 2027E/2028E (vs. high single digit % and low teens % previously).

### Page 4

Earnings changes, valuation and risks 
Forecast changes 
We revise down our 2026E EPS by -1.5% as to factor in management’s updated revenue 
growth guidance for 2H26, which came in lower vs. our prior forecasts. For 
2027E-2028E, we revise up EPS by 4.1%/6.2% mainly as to reflect our view of the 
stronger new US customer contribution, and better operating leverage as revenue 
further scales up.

Maintain Buy with TP raised to NT$8,500 from NT$8,000 
Following our upward earnings revisions, we raise our 12-month TP to NT$8,500 (from 
NT$8,000 previously). Our 12-month TP is based on a 40x 2028E discounted P/E 
(unchanged). We apply this to our 2028E EPS, and discount it back to 2027E at a 12.4% 
CoE to arrive at our TP of NT$8,000. Our key assumptions for CoE include: (1) 1.3x beta 
(sourcing Bloomberg), (2) a 4.25% risk-free rate (inline with GS house-view), and (3) a 
market risk premium of 6.25% (in line with our GS house view). This is also inline with our 
WinWay valuation methodology. Overall, our new TP implies c.29% upside from the 
current share price, and we maintain our Buy rating on MPI.

### Page 7

Investment Thesis, Price Target Risks and Methodology

Investment Thesis – MPI (6223.TWO) 
MPI is a leading Taiwan-based probe card provider specializing in CPC (Cantilever Probe 
Card), VPC (Vertical Probe Card), and MEMS (Micro-Electro-Mechanical Systems) Probe 
Card, which are used in the chip probe (CP) stage. The company is the 4th largest probe 
card supplier with c.7% market share (2024), and 57% of its total revenue comes from 
the probe card segment. 
We like MPI as we expect its revenue/earnings CAGR to further accelerate to 69%/95% 
in 2025-28E, driven by 1) continued share gain in vertical probe card (VPC) market via AI 
ASIC, and 2) potential share gain in MEMS probe card market through penetration into 
new both AI and non-AI customers, along with AI ASIC upgrade. MPI is also aiming to 
increase its PCB self-sufficiency to enable shorter product delivery lead time, which 
would provide it with more advantages over peers. The company is now trading above 
its average AI cycle forward P/E; however, with increasing ASP thanks to rising chip 
complexity and shorter product cadence driving frequent probe card upgrade demand, 
we continue to see upside potential to its revenue and earnings growth. We have a Buy 
rating on the name.  
Price Target Risks and Methodology – MPI (6223.TWO) 
Valuation: We are Buy rated on MPI. Our 12-month TP of NT$8,500 is based on a target 
P/E of 40x (1.7x of 3-year average forward P/E) applied to our 2028E EPS, and 
discounted back to 2027E at 12.4% CoE. Our key assumptions for CoE include: (1) 1.3x 
beta (sourcing Bloomberg), (2) a 4.25% risk-free rate, and (3) a market risk premium at 
6.25% (in line with GS house view). 
Key risks to our views: 1) softer AI/HPC demand, 2) slower penetration into new TAM, 
and 3) intensifying competition.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.
Coverage Universe: A list of all stocks in each coverage universe is available by primary analyst, stock and coverage universe at

https://www.gs.com/research/hedge.html.
Not Rated (NR). The investment rating, target price and earnings estimates (where relevant) are removed pursuant to Goldman Sachs policy when
Goldman Sachs is acting in an advisory capacity in a merger or in a strategic transaction involving this company, when there are legal, regulatory or
policy constraints due to Goldman Sachs’ involvement in a transaction, and in certain other circumstances. Early-Stage Biotech (ES). An investment

rating and a target price are not assigned pursuant to Goldman Sachs policy when this company has neither a drug, treatment or medical device that
has passed a Phase II clinical trial nor a license to distribute a post-Phase II drug, treatment or medical device.  Rating Suspended (RS). Goldman Sachs
Research has suspended the investment rating and price target for this stock, because there is not a sufficient fundamental basis for determining an
investment rating or target price. The previous investment rating and target price, if any, are no longer in effect for this stock and should not be relied

upon.  Coverage Suspended (CS). Goldman Sachs has suspended coverage of this company.  Not Covered (NC). Goldman Sachs does not cover this
company.

Greater China Technology Hardware
Asia Pacific
Industry View In-Line
Overall July ODM notebook shipments came in 4% below our estimate:
Component constraints remain a bottleneck, but there is evidence of a fade in the 1H
pull-forward demand. However, we do expect product mix to continue to shift
toward higher-end, more-profitable models. On their recent 2Q earnings calls, the

majority of ODMs noted  that 2H NB builds will be weaker than 1H, which is atypical
of historical seasonality (historically NB builds are 2H>1H). We forecast August NB
builds at 9.0mn (+10% q/q, -20% y/y).
We lower our 3Q notebook build estimate by 6% to 27.3mn units (-10% q/q, -
20% y/y), after factoring in latest guidance from the ODMs post 2Q earnings call.
This 10% sequential decline is sub-seasonal vs. the 15-year average seasonality of
+5% q/q in 3Q. Actual shipments will remain reliant on component availability, but

we continue to hear about constraints and expect this to continue to cap low-end
notebook model builds. Quanta commented on its recent earnings call that 2Q is
typically the peak season for Chromebooks, but due to Chromebook's target market
being more price sensitive, as well as component constraints, Chromebook demand
this year in 2Q was sub-seasonal, accounting for <10% of total notebooks builds
during the quarter.

Key Takeaways
Top five ODMs' July 2026 NB shipments were 8.2mn units (-31% m/m, -24% y/y),
4% below our estimate.
We estimate August 2026 NB builds at 9.0mn units (+10% m/m, -20% y/y).
We lower our 3Q26 NB build estimate by 6% to 27.3mn units (-10% q/q, -20% y/
y).

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

### Page 3

Alchip (3661.TW)
14 August 2026 Citi Research
3
Existing Customer Secured; Next Major CSP Opportunity Emerging  — Alchip
believes its existing CSP relationship is increasingly sticky after multiple
generations of cooperation, with the customer already assigning it the next few
generational designs and architectures. Customer concentration nevertheless
remains high, and management acknowledged that meaningful diversification
requires winning another major hyperscaler, we believe is Google as it is the biggest

AI ASIC customer with large volume.  As CSPs increasingly adopt COT and multi-
vendor sourcing, Alchip expects additional opportunities across compute-die
design, KGD and packaging integration, supporting management’s expectation for
AI-related growth broadly in line with key customers through 2029.
Maintaining Buy, TP NT$6,800 — Given higher ASP , more value added from Alchip
and also remaining strong capex expansion from its major CSP customers, we lift

our earnings projection for Alchip by 11%/15%/32% for 2026/2027 /2028. We
maintain Buy on Alchip with higher target price NT$6,800 (from NT$6,200), based
on 35x of our 2027 EPS estimate. We lower our target PER from 37x to 35x to
reflect recent market uncertainties.

### Page 7

Alchip (3661.TW)
14 August 2026 Citi Research
7
Alchip
Company description
Alchip is a leading provider of silicon design and manufacturing service,
specializing in ASIC (Application-Specific Integrated Circuit) design,
development, and production. Founded in 2003, the company has >1,000
employees and operates in Taipei, Shanghai, Japan, Korea, and the US. Alchip
serves a wide range of industries, including automotive, industrials, consumer
electronics, and computing.

Investment strategy
We rate Alchip as Buy. We expect Alchip to deliver a strong snapback in
2026/27 with its N3 project ramping up. Moreover, we are also positive over
the long-term growth opportunity from its AI ASIC projects with its major CSP
client. Alchip remains one of the few design service providers within TSMC’s
3DFabric alliance. In our view, if CSPs work more aggressively on their internal
design and become more competitive on product offerings, Alchip would be

well positioned as a back-end/turnkey partner.
Valuation
Our target price for Alchip of NT$6,800 is based on 35x 2027E EPS. 35x
target PER is largely in line with its historical 3-year PER range, which we see
as justified on account of strong growth potential of its AI ASIC projects with
its key CSP client, with better project cycle and strong growth momentum
resuming next year. Our target price equates to 2026/27E P/B of 11x/8x.
Risks
Key downside risks to our target price include: 1) slower-than-expected NRE

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds

2Q26 miss: Auras’ 2Q26 revenue was largely in line with our estimate and Bloomberg 
consensus, while GM was QoQ down, which we attribute to the trial production of 
new liquid cooling components for next-generation ASIC and GPU AI servers. Despite 
the GM miss in 2Q26, we expect GM to improve in 2H26 driven by better product 
mix toward liquid cooling components. 2Q26 opex ratio was lower than our estimate, 
reflecting operational efficiency improvement; along with higher than expected tax 
expense, this led 2Q26 net income to come in 23% / 21% below our estimate / 
Bloomberg consensus.

Earnings revision: We factor in Auras’ Jul revenue and 2Q26 result and raise our 
2026-28E revenues by 5% / 7% / 11%, mainly on higher AI server liquid cooling 
component shipments, benefiting from the rising liquid cooling solution penetration 
rate and dollar content increase in AI servers. We slightly cut our 2026E GM to reflect 
the impact of trail production in 2Q26 and keep our 2027-28E GMs largely 
unchanged. Our estimates still show a GM uptrend in coming years, reflecting our

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Auras 
Our 12m target price for Auras is based on a near-term P/E, consistent with our Taiwan 
Technology coverage. We base our 12-m TP of NT$1,353 on a target P/E multiple of 
19.4x forward year EPS (2027E). Our target P/E is derived from the correlation between 
P/E and EPS growth of its peers in technology hardware, based on the company’s 
2027E-28E EPS YoY growth at 23%. Key downside risks: 1) Slower-than-expected liquid 
cooling penetration; (2) Soft AI server demand; (3) Weaker-than-expected PC market; 
and (4) Potential competition from existing and new suppliers.

eMemory Technology Inc  | Asia Pacific
An overlooked quantum and IP
play; Initiate at OW
We expect eMemory to benefit as PUF-PQC adoption appears
set to accelerate, driven by NeoFuse adoption, PQC
opportunities, and royalty growth.
eMemory is a leading embedded non-volatile memory (eNVM) and hardware
security IP provider with a royalty-driven business model: The company
generates high-margin recurring royalty revenue through its semiconductor IP

portfolio. We believe eMemory is well positioned to benefit from advanced-node
migration and the emerging post-quantum cryptography (PQC) opportunity (see our
report: Quantum Security – Building Defenses for Q-Day and Beyond), supporting
long-term royalty growth.
Well positioned for advanced-node migration, supporting broader NeoFuse OTP
(one-time programmable memory) adoption: We believe continued migration
toward advanced process technologies is likely to  drive NeoFuse adoption. Higher

wafer values at advanced nodes should further support royalty growth while
creating future NeoPUF (physical unclonable function) opportunities.
Emerging PQC opportunity could create a second long-term royalty engine: As
governments and enterprises transition toward quantum-safe security, hardware-
based key protection is becoming increasingly important. We believe NeoPUF  is well
positioned as a hardware root-of-trust (RoT) solution and could become a

meaningful growth driver alongside OTP.
Initiate at Overweight with a NT$3,330 price target: Our valuation is based on a
residual income model. We forecast a 25% EPS CAGR (2025-28E), driven by royalty
growth, NeoFuse adoption, and security IP.
Key risks to our outlook include: 1) slower-than-expected adoption by key
customers, 2) slower-than-expected PQC adoption, and 3) competition in the PUF-
based PQC solution.

Morgan Stanley Research 5
Investment Summary: An overlooked quantum and IP play
eMemory is a leading provider of embedded non-volatile memory (eNVM) and hardware
security IP, with a business model differentiated by its high-margin, recurring royalty
revenue stream. In our view, the market continues to primarily view eMemory as an OTP
IP provider, with limited recognition of the company's potential exposure to emerging
post-quantum security opportunities. We believe the company is well positioned to

benefit from two structural trends: (1) continued migration toward advanced
semiconductor nodes, which should support broader adoption of NeoFuse OTP solutions,
and (2) the emergence of PQC, which could create a new long-term growth opportunity
for NeoPUF. As governments and enterprises accelerate migration toward quantum-safe
security architectures, we believe hardware-based key protection will become increasingly
important. eMemory's NeoFuse-based PUF technology is well positioned to serve as a

hardware root of trust for PQC-enabled systems. While PUF currently contributes only
~1% of the company's royalties, broader adoption could create a second long-term royalty
engine alongside eMemory's traditional OTP business. In addition, we view NeoFuse as
positioned to benefit from advanced-node migration, while potentially creating pull-
through opportunities for future NeoPUF adoption. In our view, given eMemory's IP-
centric business model, rising wafer values and increasing production volumes at advanced

nodes should also support sustained royalty expansion over time.
NeoPUF may offer advantages in advanced-node scaling, low-
power designs, and next-generation hardware root-of-trust
applications...
Among today's hardware root-of-trust makers, Synopsys is currently the most direct
competitor to eMemory. Following its March 2024 acquisition of Intrinsic ID, Synopsys'
security platform is primarily based on SRAM PUF technology, which derives device-

unique cryptographic keys from the start-up behavior of volatile SRAM cells. From a
system design perspective, SRAM PUF solutions require dedicated SRAM resources and
additional stabilization, helper-data, and error correcting code (ECC) circuitry to
reconstruct stable keys across variations in temperature, voltage, and device aging.
Unlike SRAM PUF, which derives entropy from the power-up behavior of memory cells,
NeoPUF generates its fingerprint from quantum tunneling characteristics created by

randomly distributed oxide defects within the gate oxide. These defects are formed
naturally during semiconductor manufacturing and produce an enormous number of
possible device states, enabling each chip to generate a highly unique and statistically
random silicon fingerprint with randomness approaching the theoretical ideal.
More importantly, NeoPUF's near-zero bit-error rate originates from the permanent nature
of the underlying oxide defects and dangling bonds. Once these atomic-scale defects are

formed during fabrication, they become highly stable physical structures. Reconstructing
the original silicon-oxide bonding network would require temperatures exceeding
approximately 700°C, far beyond any normal operating or qualification condition. As a
result, the tunneling characteristics remain essentially unchanged throughout the lifetime
of the device, allowing the same enrolled root key to be regenerated repeatedly with
virtually identical responses years later.

### Page 6

M
Foundation
6
Exhibit 8: NeoPUF vs. SRAM PUF
Source: PUFsecurity
We believe eMemory's moat extends beyond NeoPUF itself. The company has spent years
developing and validating its security IP portfolio, supported by a broad patent base and
expertise accumulated through multiple technology generations. Given the mission-critical
nature of hardware root-of-trust architectures, customers tend to prioritize proven
reliability over adopting new security technologies, resulting in long qualification cycles

and high switching costs.
OTP Business Benefits from Advanced-Node Migration and
Rising Wafer Values
As semiconductor production continues to migrate toward advanced process nodes, OTP
benefits from increasing eMemory's penetration as conventional eFuse solutions face
growing scaling challenges at smaller geometries. At the same time, royalties account for
the majority of eMemory's earnings and are closely tied to customer production. As
manufacturing shifts toward leading-edge nodes, higher wafer values translate into higher

royalty revenue per design win. In addition, customers upgrading their security
architecture at advanced nodes are increasingly evaluating OTP and PUF solutions
together, creating incremental cross-selling opportunities. Consequently, we believe that
advanced-node migration should support both broader OTP deployment and sustained
royalty growth over the long term.

Investment Thesis
Recent correction creates an attractive entry point: We believe the recent share price
correction was triggered by 1Q26 earnings miss and concerns over near-term OTP royalty
growth. However, we view part of the weakness as timing-related, as 1Q26 royalties had
yet to reflect recent foundry price increases that management expects to benefit results
in 2H26. More importantly, investor attention remains almost entirely focused on the core

OTP business, while assigning little value to NeoPUF and eMemory's positioning within
the emerging post-quantum security ecosystem.
The market is overlooking eMemory's long-standing PUF investment: We believe
investors continue to view eMemory primarily as an OTP IP company, overlooking its
multi-year investment in PUF technology. Unlike many companies that only recently
began positioning around post-quantum security, eMemory has been developing NeoPUF

for years and has already secured commercial deployments that contribute revenue today.
Attractive valuation relative to both security and semiconductor IP peers: We believe
eMemory should be assessed through both the quantum security and semiconductor IP
peer groups given its exposure to hardware security and royalty-driven IP licensing.
Relative to quantum security peers, eMemory stands out as one of the few companies
generating meaningful earnings and cash flow. Relative to semiconductor IP peers, its

valuation does not appear demanding despite offering superior profitability, with above
50% ROE, well above most global IP peers. We therefore believe the company's valuation
premium is supported by its earnings quality and business model.
Why we rate the shares OW: We see a differentiated combination of structural royalty
growth, advanced-node migration exposure, and underappreciated post-quantum security
optionality. While the market primarily values eMemory based on its established OTP

franchise, we believe NeoPUF could emerge as a meaningful second growth engine as
hardware root-of-trust adoption expands in the post-quantum era. As a result, we see the
potential for both earnings growth and a gradual re-rating of the stock over time.
Investment Positives
1.    PQC adoption could create a new long-term growth engine for PUF: As
governments and enterprises accelerate the transition toward PQC, hardware-
based key protection is becoming increasingly important. PUF is emerging as a

critical hardware root-of-trust component, enabling secure generation and
protection of large cryptographic keys required by PQC architectures. While PUF
currently contributes only around 1%  revenue for eMemory, we believe adoption
could accelerate meaningfully over the next few years as security requirements
evolve. More importantly, eMemory's IP business model benefits from strong
customer stickiness. As a result, broader PUF adoption has the potential to

become a structural growth driver rather than a one-off licensing opportunity,
creating a second long-term royalty engine alongside OTP.
2. NeoFuse is positioned to benefit from advanced-node migration, while

### Page 9

M
Foundation
Morgan Stanley Research 9
creating additional pull-through opportunities for NeoPUF: As semiconductor
geometries continue to shrink, conventional eFuse solutions face increasing
scaling challenges related to area efficiency, reliability and process compatibility.
NeoFuse offers an alternative OTP architecture that is designed to be more
suitable for advanced nodes, particularly below 28nm, where customers have
fewer commercially available options. We believe this creates a favorable

competitive position for eMemory, allowing the company to expand OTP
penetration as advanced-node adoption increases. In addition, OTP and PUF are
becoming increasingly complementary rather than standalone solutions. As
customers redesign their security architecture around next-generation OTP
solutions, they are more likely to evaluate PUF at the same decision point,
positioning eMemory to benefit from its integrated OTP and PUF portfolio. This
creates a potential cross-selling opportunity, where broader NeoFuse deployment

not only can expand OTP royalties but could also increase future PUF royalties. In
our view, OTP migration may therefore also serve as an important catalyst for
future PUF adoption.
3. Higher wafer values could drive sustained royalty expansion: Unlike many IP
vendors that primarily rely on upfront licensing revenue, eMemory generates
about 70% of its earnings from royalties tied to customer production. As
semiconductor content continues to migrate toward more advanced process

nodes, wafer costs have increased substantially versus mature-node production.
Because royalty payments are generally linked to wafer utilization and production
volume, rising wafer values can translate into higher royalty revenue per design
win. This dynamic creates a favorable business model characteristic: once IP has
been qualified and designed into a product, eMemory can continue to participate
in customers' production growth with limited incremental operating costs.

Therefore,  continued migration toward advanced nodes and higher wafer ASPs
could support long-term royalty growth and operating leverage.
Investment Concerns
1.     Slower-than-expected adoption by key customers: Leading semiconductor
companies tend to be more conservative when evaluating and deploying new
memory and security architectures due to extensive validation and qualification
requirements. Given the increasingly concentrated nature of semiconductor

shipments, delayed adoption by a handful of major customers could slow
eMemory's revenue penetration at foundries and postpone the ramp-up of royalty
revenue.
2. Slower-than-expected PQC adoption:      If enterprise, cloud, automotive, or
government adoption of PQC progresses more slowly than expected, demand for
PUF-based security solutions may take longer to materialize, delaying NeoPUF's
revenue contribution and associated royalty growth.
3. Geopolitical and trade policy risks: Rising geopolitical risks could become an

additional consideration in customers' technology sourcing decisions. This may
affect the pace of design-win conversion and customer adoption, particularly

### Page 10

M
Foundation
10
among global customers with increasingly diversified supply-chain strategies.

eMemory: Earnings  Outlook
Revenue
We expect eMemory's revenue to grow at a 25.9% CAGR over 2025-28E, driven by rising
OTP penetration in advanced semiconductor applications, continued ASP increases across
its IP portfolio, and an expanding royalty base. We also expect growing adoption of
advanced-node OTP solutions to support both licensing and recurring royalty revenue,
while PUF-related products begin contributing meaningfully from 2027 onwards as

hardware security requirements increase across AI, data center, automotive, and industrial
applications. Looking further ahead, we see PUF and post-quantum security-related
opportunities as emerging growth drivers for eMemory. While commercialization remains
at an early stage, increasing demand for hardware root-of-trust architectures and secure
device authentication should gradually expand the addressable market and support a
higher contribution from security-related IP over time.

EPS
We expect eMemory's EPS to increase from NT$25.99 in 2025 to NT$53.31 in 2028,
representing a 27% CAGR over 2025-28E. Earnings growth should be driven by continued
revenue expansion, a growing mix of higher-margin royalty revenue, and the company's
highly scalable licensing model. Given its asset-light business model and minimal cost of
goods sold, we expect much of the incremental revenue to flow through to the bottom
line, supporting sustained profitability and earnings growth.

How our estimates differ from consensus...
Our 2026-28E EPS estimates are below consensus. As eMemory remains relatively under-
covered by the sell-side, consensus estimates are derived from a relatively small analyst
base. While we remain positive on the company's long-term growth prospects, our
forecasts assume a more gradual earnings trajectory over the forecast period, resulting in
earnings estimates below current consensus expectations.

eMemory: Valuation methodology
Our price target is NT$3,330: We derive our price target from a residual income model,
as we believe this methodology is best suited to capturing the long-term value of
eMemory's high-margin IP and royalty business model. We assume an 8.6% cost of equity
(2.0% risk-free rate, 6.0% equity risk premium, and 1.1 beta), a payout ratio of 90%, a
medium-term earnings growth rate of 23.0%, and a terminal growth rate of 4.4%. Based

on these assumptions, our RI model implies an equity value of NT$249bn, or NT$3,330
per share.
Our price target implies 80x 2027e P/E, above the company's historical average of 69x but
below its +1SD valuation band of 97x. We believe this  valuation is warranted by
eMemory's leadership in embedded non-volatile memory and hardware security IP, long-
duration royalty streams, and growing exposure to secular semiconductor security trends,
including post-quantum cryptography and hardware root of trust.

We believe eMemory should be evaluated through both the quantum computing/security
and semiconductor IP peer groups, given its exposure to both hardware security and
semiconductor royalty-driven business models. Relative to quantum and security peers,
eMemory appears attractive on a P/S basis while standing out as one of the few
companies generating meaningful earnings and cash flow. Relative to semiconductor IP
peers, we view Arm as the most relevant benchmark given the similarities in licensing and

royalty business models. Compared with Arm, eMemory's valuation does not appear
particularly demanding despite delivering significantly higher profitability. With a 2027E
return on average equity (ROAE) of 61%, nearly double that of most global IP peers, we
believe eMemory's valuation premium is justified by its superior earnings quality.
Scenario Analysis
Base case: We forecast revenue CAGR of 25.9% over 2025-28E, driven by rising OTP
penetration in advanced semiconductor applications, continued ASP increases across its IP

portfolio, and an expanding royalty base. Under these assumptions, we derive our base-
case valuation of NT$3,330/share.
Bull case: We forecast revenue CAGR of 42% over 2025-28E, supported by successful
NeoPUF adoption among major hyperscale and semiconductor customers. We assume
eMemory expands its penetration into datacenter and AI-related chips, while advanced-
node OTP adoption and royalty growth exceed our base-case expectations. Under this
scenario, we derive our bull-case valuation of NT$4,555/share.

Bear case: We forecast revenue CAGR of 5% over 2025-28E, reflecting slower-than-
expected OTP adoption at advanced nodes. We also assume alternative PUF technologies
gain market traction, limiting NeoPUF penetration and resulting in weaker royalty growth.
Under this scenario, we derive our bear-case valuation of NT$1,865/share.

Company Background
eMemory Technology Inc., founded in 2000 and headquartered in Hsinchu, Taiwan, is a
leading semiconductor IP provider specializing in eNVM and hardware security
technologies. The company develops and licenses a broad portfolio of memory IP
solutions, including OTP, MTP, flash, PUF, and emerging memory technologies, serving
applications across AI, data centers, networking, automotive, industrial, and IoT markets.

With more than 1,350 patents and technology support extending from mature nodes to
advanced 2nm processes, eMemory has established itself as one of the world's leading
memory IP vendors.
The company's core business is built on embedded memory IP solutions, including OTP,
MTP, and flash technologies, which are widely used for code storage, device configuration,
calibration, and security applications across a broad range of semiconductor devices.
Leveraging its long-standing expertise in embedded memory technologies, eMemory has

gradually expanded its product portfolio to include NeoPUF, NeoFuse, NeoEE, NeoMTP,
NeoFlash, and other next-generation memory and security solutions. These products
target growing demand for advanced semiconductor security, AI infrastructure,
automotive electronics, and future PQC-enabled systems.
The company generated 67.5% of FY25 revenue from recurring royalty income and 32.5%
from license fees, reflecting the scalability of its IP licensing business model.

Key Competitors
Globally, eMemory competes with a range of embedded non-volatile memory (eNVM) and
hardware security IP providers. In the OTP and eNVM IP market, Synopsys (through its

Kilopass acquisition) is generally considered one of the company's closest competitors,
while broader semiconductor IP vendors such as Cadence also participate in selected
overlapping markets. In security-related applications, competition comes from companies
including Rambus, Secure-IC and Intrinsic ID, which offer hardware security, root-of-trust
and PUF-based solutions. In Taiwan, companies such as Faraday and M31 have exposure to

### Page 18

M
Foundation
18
selected memory and security IP markets, although their business focus differs from
eMemory's core offerings. The market remains highly fragmented, with competitive
dynamics varying across process technologies, end-applications and customer
requirements, while foundry-provided memory solutions and internally developed IP at
large semiconductor companies also represent important alternative offerings.
Key Suppliers and Customers
eMemory's customers primarily consist of leading global fabless semiconductor

companies, IDMs and memory manufacturers across consumer, communications, data
center and automotive markets. Revenue is generated through a combination of upfront
licensing fees and recurring royalties. Customers first adopt eMemory's IP during the
design stage and subsequently enter mass production through qualified foundries. Royalty
revenue is then collected based on wafer shipments incorporating eMemory's IP, primarily
from foundries such as TSMC and UMC.

Shareholding Structure
As of April 2026, eMemory's shareholder base was primarily composed of long-term
institutional investors. The largest shareholder was Citibank Custody Singapore
Government Investment Corporation (GIC), with a 6.23% stake, followed by Standard
Chartered Custody for Smallcap World Fund, with 5.45%. Other major shareholders
included Fubon Life Insurance (3.89%), the Labor Pension Fund (3.78%), and board
member Chen Li-Chuan (2.97%). The top ten shareholders were predominantly global

institutional investors and domestic financial institutions, reflecting a relatively diversified
ownership structure with no single shareholder holding a controlling stake.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

Update
Innolux  | Asia Pacific
Sub-seasonal 2H26; Glass in
Advanced Packaging  Awaiting
More Meaningful Contribution
We maintain the view that it is still premature to accumulate
shares from the angle of glass opportunities in advanced
packaging. Stay EW.
Core business update and outlook:
• Commodity display: TV demand should be relatively stable, but earlier
restocking took place in 1H26. Meanwhile, NBs are suffering from higher
component costs.

• Non-commodity display: It sees good synergies from the Pioneer acquisition
and expects benefits to emerge in the future.
• Non Display: Demand is relatively stable and should continue to grow.
Emerging semiconductor business:
• Chip first offering should  grow shipments by double digits YoY this year.
• Continued to work with partner on TGV; likely to see adoption in 2028.
• No comment on capex or capacity plans at this stage, but it will try to keep

both under control.
• Despite the possibility of more players joining the supply chain, it is
confident about its own first-mover advantage.
Fab disposal:
• No plan for aggressive fab disposal in the future; instead, will focus more on
the production facility optimization.
Our view:
• We think the market's focus is still on glass opportunities in advanced
packaging, although we sense that the enthusiasm seems to have normalized
in the past few months, before the supply chain sees major breakthrough in

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Innolux (3481.TW)
Base case, 2.1x 2026e P/B. We use price/book value as our methodology because we believe
it better reflects Innolux's intrinsic value, in view of the industry's relatively cyclical nature.
Innolux has generally traded at an average of 0.6x P/B, but the stock has re-rated from the
FOPLP/advanced packaging angle, We view 2.1x as justified because we see do not see semi

contribution kicking in until 2028.
Risks to Upside
n More panel price hikes amid slower ramps of Chinese players
n Higher contribution from assembly business
n Stronger TV/IT demand recovery
n Faster progress on semi businesses
Risks to Downside
n Macro demand slowdown
n Fiercer competition from Chinese panel makers
n Faster OLED panel adoptions in large size applications
n Slower progress on semi businesses

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

SCFI 大漲 ， 等 同 暗 示 陽明  3Q 旺季  營運將 大 幅 跳 升。 
四.美國  企 業 存 貨 銷 售 比 創 54 個 月 新 低 ， 補 貨 力 道 推 升 運 價

含 製 造 、 躉 售 、 零 售 ） ， 攀 升 至 1.74 ，再下 滑 至 2021 年 6 月
1.26 ，補 貨 需 求 激 勵 貨 櫃運 價 上 升，2022 年 1 月 SCFI 貨 櫃 運 價
指數  5,109.6 ，創歷 史 新 高； 陽明  2022 年稅後 EPS  + 51.71 元。 
【目前狀況 】：2026 年 7 月 16 日 發 布 最 新 2026 年 5 月 美 國 企
業 存 貨 銷 售 比 1.28 ，創  54 個 月 新 低 （202 1 年 12 月 ~ 2026 年
5 月 ） ； 凸 顯 美 國 企 業 補貨力 道 將 轉 強； 一 路 下 滑 的 企 業 存
貨 銷 售 比 （參閱 圖 2），成 為 推 升 SCFI 貨 櫃 運 價 指 數的 關 鍵 力
量。隨著 12 月 聖 誕 季 逼 近，美 國 企 業 的 補 貨 力 道 將 持 續 轉 強。

貳、公 司 概 況 
一.公司簡介  
陽 明，全 球 第 9 大 貨 櫃 航 商，以 遠 洋 貨 櫃 航 線（ 歐 洲 線；美 西 線 ；
美 東 線 ）為主 ， 以 近 洋 航 線 （ 亞 洲 線 ） 為 輔。 
2025 年 營 收 比 重 ： 歐 洲 線38%； 美 洲 線 36%； 亞 洲 線 12%； 
其他 14%（ 中 東 、 南 美 、 澳 洲 ） 。

目 前 船 隊 （ 截 至202 6 年 8 月 13 日 ； 依 據Alphaliner 統 計 資 料）： 
總 營 運 艘 數 99 艘，總 營 運 TEU 數為  757,240 TEU，占 全 球 貨 櫃 船
隊 運 力 2.2%： 自 有 ：63 艘 ， 總 TEU 數 382,296  TEU 。 
租 賃 ：36 艘，總 TEU 數 374,944  TEU 。

訂 船 數 量： 待 交 船 數 21 艘 ， 合 計 268 ,360  TEU ， 
約 當 目 前 船 隊運力  35.4%。

二.營運概況及展望  
1. 美國  企 業 存 貨 銷 售 比 創 54 個 月 新 低 ， 運 價 易 漲 難 跌 
美國  企 業 存 貨 銷 售 比 （ 含 製 造 業 、 躉 售 業 、 零 售 業 ） ， 顯 示 相
對 於 銷 售 ， 企 業 存 貨 的 相 對 應 付 能 力 （ 等 同 於 存貨  還 能 應 付
多 久 的 銷 售 需 求 ） ：

企 業 存 貨 銷 售 比 走 高 ， 顯 示 存 貨 過 多 ， 去 化 壓 力 上 升 。 
企 業 存 貨 銷 售 比 走 低 ， 顯 示 存貨 不足 ， 補 貨 需 求 上 升。

受到  新 冠 疫 情 影 響 （2019 年 12 月 新 冠 疫 情 出 現 ，2020 年 3 月
全 球 大 流 行 ） ，美國  2020 年 4 月 企 業 存 貨 銷 售 比（參閱圖 2），
攀升至  1.74 ， 後 續， 一 路 下 滑 至 2021 年 6 月 1.26 ， 帶 來 強 大 的
補 貨 力道 ，美 國 進 口 貨 物 需 求 大 增，海 運 需 求 及 運 價 同 步 上 升 ，
SCFI 貨櫃運價指數在  2022 年 1 月攀升至  5,109.6 的歷史新高水
準 ； 貨 櫃 航 商 陽明 海運  2022 年稅後 EPS 攀升至  + 51. 71 元 。

圖 2：美國企業存貨銷售比（含 製造業、躉售業、零售業）創 54 個月新低； 補貨力道將推升運價

偏重美國市場 的 傳統產業 與 貨櫃航運產業 將受惠
2021年6月 美國 企業存貨銷售比 降至 1.26
激勵2022年1月SCFI貨櫃運價創歷史新高 5,109.6
目前 2026年 5月 最新 美國企業存貨銷售比
明顯走低至 1.28，創最近 54個月新低
有利於推升長期貨櫃運價 。
附註：企業存貨銷售比，包含 製造業、 躉售業 、 零售業 。
